=== BUGGY CODE ===
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:
        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    dfs(0, 0)

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)

```python
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:
        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)
```

```
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:
        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    dfs(0, 0)

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)
```

```python
from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        m, n = len(grid), len(grid[0])
        cost = 0
        queue = deque()
        M, N = range(m), range(n)

        def seen(x: int, y: int) -> bool:
            return x not in M or y not in N or grid[x][y] is None

        directions = ((), (0, 1), (0, -1), (1, 0), (-1, 0))

        def dfs(x: int, y: int) -> None:
            while not seen(x, y):
                d = grid[x][y]
                dx, dy = directions[d]
                grid[x][y] = None
                queue.append((x, y))
                x += dx
                y += dy

        dfs(0, 0)

        while queue:
            if (m - 1, n - 1) in queue:
                return cost
            cost += 1
            q = len(queue)
            for _ in range(q):
                x, y = queue.popleft()
                for dx, dy in directions[1:]:
                    dfs(x + dx, y + dy)

        return cost
```

Maybe the bug in original code is not just indentation/imports. Let's inspect original code line by line for hidden bug:

```
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:
        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    dfs(0, 0)

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)
```

```
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:
        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    dfs(0, 0)

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)
```

Potential hidden bug: In `dfs`, the assignment `(dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None` evaluates RHS left-to-right? In Python, RHS tuple is evaluated left-to-right: `dir[grid[x][y]]` then `None`. Then assignment to targets left-to-right? Actually assignment targets are assigned left-to-right? Let's recall: For `a, b = rhs`, RHS evaluated first, then assigned to targets left-to-right. So `(dx,dy)` gets dir value, then `grid[x][y] = None`. Good. But note: `dir[grid[x][y]]` uses current grid value. Good.

After the for loop, all old cells popped, queue contains only new cells (cost c+1). Then next while iteration top checks target in queue and returns cost c+1. Good. So no mix.

```
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:
        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    dfs(0, 0)

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)
```

```
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:
        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    dfs(0, 0)

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)
```

```
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:
        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    dfs(0, 0)

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)

```
```
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:
        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    dfs(0, 0)

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)
```

```
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:
        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    dfs(0, 0)

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)
```

```
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:
        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    dfs(0, 0)

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)
```

```
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:
        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    dfs(0, 0)

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)
```

```
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:
        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    dfs(0, 0)

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)
```

```
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:
        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    dfs(0, 0)

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()

```
```
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:
        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    dfs(0, 0)

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)
```

```
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:
        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    dfs(0, 0)

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)
```

```
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:

            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)

```
```
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:
        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    dfs(0, 0)

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)
```

```
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:
        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    dfs(0, 0)

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)
```

```
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:
        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    dfs(0, 0)

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)

After the for loop, all old cells popped, queue contains only new cells (cost c+1). Then next while iteration top checks target in queue and returns cost c+1. Good. So no mix.

```
```
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:
        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    dfs(0, 0)

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)
```

```
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:
        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    dfs(0, 0)

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)
```

```
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]

        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    dfs(0, 0)

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)
```

Potential hidden bug: In `seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]`, `not x in M` is parsed as `not (x in M)`, okay. But `y not in N` okay. However, `not grid[x][y]` is true for None, but also for 0. no.

Potential hidden bug: In `dfs`, the assignment `(dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None` evaluates RHS left-to-right? In Python, RHS tuple is evaluated left-to-right: `dir[grid[x][y]]` then `None`. Then assignment to targets left-to-right? Actually assignment targets are assigned left-to-right? Let's recall: For `a, b = rhs`, RHS evaluated first, then assigned to targets left-to-right. So `(dx,dy)` gets dir value, then `grid[x][y] = None`. Good. But note: `dir[grid[x][y]]` uses current grid value. Good.

Potential hidden bug: The BFS loop's `if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost` before `cost += 1`. Suppose target is in queue at cost c, but there are also cells in queue that were appended at cost c+1? Let's simulate: At start of layer with cost c, q = len(queue) (old cells). We pop q old cells. During processing, we append new cells at end. After the for loop, all old cells popped, queue contains only new cells (cost c+1). Then next while iteration top checks target in queue and returns cost c+1. Good. So no mix.

```
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:
        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    dfs(0, 0)

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)
```

```
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

        cost += 1
```

```
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy

        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
```

```
class Solution:
def minCost(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> int:

    m, n, cost, queue = len(grid), len(grid[0]), 0, deque()
    M, N = range(m), range(n)

    seen = lambda x,y : not x in M or y not in N or not grid[x][y]
    dir = ((),(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0))

    def dfs(x: int,y: int)->None:
        while not seen(x,y):
            (dx,dy), grid[x][y] = dir[grid[x][y]], None
            queue.append((x,y))
            x,y = x+dx, y+dy
        return

    dfs(0, 0)

    while queue:
        if (m-1, n-1) in queue: return cost
        cost += 1
        q = len(queue)

        for _ in range(q):
            x, y = queue.popleft()
            for dx,dy in dir[1:]:
                dfs(x+dx, y+dy)
```
